Pairwise swap/move: Since sum constraint, we can transfer mass ε from coordinate p to q: h_p -= ε, h_q += ε, preserve bounds. Objective as function along ε is max of quadratics? Feasibility projection via water-filling: Need handle target n/2. Implementation:
```
def project_feasible(v, n):
    target = n/2.0
    lo = v.min() - 1.0
    hi = v.max() + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(v - mid,0,1).sum()
       if s > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    x=np.clip(v-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
    # fix tiny sum:
    x += (target - x.sum())/n # but may violate tiny; clip and renormalize?
    x = np.clip(x,0,1)
    # iterative last
    for _ in range(20): ...
    return x
```
Since v target within [0,n], the bisection works. At end clip. To correct residual, add tiny to interior. Use previous project iterative fallback.

def c5_bound(h,n): return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')))*2/n
```

Let's design specific algorithms and hyperparameters.

A. `optimize_block(h,n,deadline,seed)` performs alternating LP and subgrad, eps adaptation.
Pseudo:
```
def optimize_block(h, n, t0, deadline, seed, eps=0.05, use_lp=True, use_sg=True):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h; best_c=c5_bound(h,n)
    noimp=0
    while time.time()<deadline-2 and noimp<...:
        if use_lp:
            eps_try=eps
            h2,c2 = lp_step(h,n,eps_try,K=120)
        else: h2=h
        if c2>best_c*0? maybe use h2
        # subgradient several
        for _ in range(5):
            h3 = subgrad_step(h2,n,beta=30,step_min...)
            if improved: update
        # if no improvement after lp+sg: reduce eps, add noise
    return best_h,best_c
```
But LP step solving linprog may be bottleneck; if too many, time. We can set iterations with time.

Maybe `lp_step` with linprog should be called only if current h not too local? Could be 100 calls. Each 0.1-0.2s =>20s. Subgrad can run 1000s fast.

`subgrad_step` could itself be called many times. We need make it efficient. We can compute C each time (np.correlate) ~0.1ms? For n=400, 160k multiplications, in C maybe 0.05ms; Python overhead maybe 0.1. 100k calls 10s. Good.

But subgrad_step with K active and line search multiple evals (say 5) => 5 correlate per step. 1000 steps =>5ms? Wait 5*1000*0.1ms=0.5s, very fast. We can do many. But numpy correlate O(n^2) for n=400 maybe 160k ~0.1ms? Could be 1ms, still 5s per 1000. Fine.

Need ensure subgradient not stuck: use variable beta and step. We can implement an internal loop with annealing:
```
def sg_loop(h,n,deadline,seed, betas=[5,10,20,50,100], steps=[...]):
   h=project; best=...
   for phase beta in betas:
      for iter until deadline:
          C=...; mask top 30? weights...
          g=...
          d=-g/norm; add small random
          for st in steps: h_try=project(h+st*d); choose best among 5; update h,best
          if no improvement many: break phase
```
This is fast and can polish.

But subgradient of max may have zero gradient for unique active at optimum? If active unique, direction from gradient can still reduce? At local min max, active set and KKT. Subgradient descent might find.

Could implement "exact line search along negative gradient" by evaluating f(h + α d) for α in [0.001,0.002,...]. Since d normalized, steps in h units. Use maybe 10 candidates. Accept any improvement. If no improvement, reduce step. To escape, random restarts.

B. `binary_search` (swaps): Good for near-binary. Let's design an efficient heuristic.
- Convert h to bool b = h>0.5, ensure exactly n/2 ones by threshold sorted: `idx=np.argsort(h); n1=n//2; b[idx[:n1]]=0; b[idx[n1:]]=1`. This yields binary from continuous.
- Objective unscaled max. We can do variable neighborhood local search:
   * Identify lags where C>current*0.98 or above min of max. But if we try direct descent, need find a swap that lowers max. We can use simulated annealing with temperature: start T small? Since target close, we need precise.
   * For each iteration, propose a swap between a 1 and a 0. Rejection if max increases <=? Use SA: accept if Δ<0 or rand<exp(-Δ/T). Δ in scaled C? Use unscaled, T ~0.1 counts.
   * To propose, sample one current one index and one zero index uniformly; but n=400, many. To focus, sample 1-index uniformly, zero-index uniformly. Evaluate new C by update? Full correlate each proposal expensive but okay for 20k proposals. Time: 20k*1ms=20s. Could do 100k in 100s.
   * We can improve by computing C array for current and updating after swap in O(n) using difference formula, instead of full correlate O(n^2)! That would allow millions. Let's derive update for swapping h_p (0->1) and h_q (1->0) in the actual correlate array. Let old C (unscaled length L=2n-1). For each index m (lag l=(n-1)-m), C_m=Σ_i h_i b_{i+l}. If h changes at p and q. We can update all m? Need O(n) per swap: For coordinate p change Δ_p=+1, for q Δ_q=-1. Derivative for each lag with respect to coordinate j is grad_l[j]. So new C_m = old C_m + Δ_p grad_{l_m}[p] + Δ_q grad_{l_m}[q] + second-order if p and q both in same term? Wait if both coordinates change, the bilinear term includes p and q if lag l = q-p or p-q; then derivative sum linear approx misses δ_p δ_q? Since old term h_p b_q? Need exact update.
   For swap, we can compute exact by considering pairs (i,j) where i or j changed with lag j-i? Let's derive with actual C_m =Σ_i h_i (1-h_{i+l}), l=(n-1)-m. A change in h_p affects terms where i=p or i+l=p (i=p-l). Terms:
   - i=p: contribution h_p(1-h_{p+l}) if valid p+l<n. Change Δ_p*(1 - old_h_{p+l}) plus if p+l=q, product with q's change: Δ_p*(-Δ_q) = -Δ_pΔ_q? Since old_h_{p+l} changes.
   - i=p-l: term h_{p-l}(1-h_p), change -Δ_p * old_h_{p-l} plus if p-l=q, +Δ_pΔ_q? Because 1-h_p change -Δ_p, multiply h_q change Δ_q gives +? Full product (h_i - h_i h_j). Let's do general pair update for changes Δ_r. C=Σ_i h_i -Σ_i h_i h_{i+l}. Change = Σ_i Δ_i -Σ_i (Δ_i h_{i+l}^{old} + h_i^{old} Δ_{i+l} + Δ_i Δ_{i+l}). Summed over i. But easier: For each lag, exact effect from two changes can be computed with formula using old h and lags d=q-p. There are at most 4 lag indices affected for each coordinate change linear plus one lag for product between p and q? Let's derive:

Fast update code details:
```
def swap_trial(C, h, omh, p, q, n):
    L=2*n-1
    tri = C.copy()
    tri[p:n+p] += omh[::-1]
    tri[n-1-p:2*n-1-p] -= h
    tri[q:n+q] -= omh[::-1]  # because subtract grad q
    tri[n-1-q:2*n-1-q] += h
    tri[n-1] -= 2.0
    d = q - p
    m1 = n - 1 - d
    if 0 <= m1 < L: tri[m1] += 1.0
    m2 = n - 1 + d
    if 0 <= m2 < L: tri[m2] += 1.0
    return tri
```
Check slices for p=q? Not used. Bounds: p:n+p length n? p max n-1, stop 2n-1, okay within L. start p first term: length n? p:n+p, if p=n-1 stop 2n-1 length n. Good. second start n-1-p >=0, stop 2n-1-p <=2n-1. For q similar.
But note when p or q near edges, slices length n within L? For p=0, first 0:n, second n-1:2n-1 length n; overlap at index n-1? first 0..n-1, second n-1..; both include n-1, correct for l=0 derivative. Good.
One_minus reversed is length n. In update, `omh[::-1]` each slice. This creates view. Good.

Targeted pair selection:
```
def targeted_pair(b, C, n, rng):
   # pick top lags where C near max
   maxc = C.max()
   # choose index among top K by probabilities? Use argrel? Simpler: top_indices = np.argsort(C)[-20:]; m = top_indices[rng.randint(20)]
   l = n - 1 - m
   if l >=0:
       # condition b[i]=1, b[i+l]=0 for i=0..n-1-l
       if l==0: return None # no self swap
       mask = (b[:n-l] == 1) & (b[l:n] == 0)
   else:
       a=-l
       # i=a..n-1 condition b[i]=1, b[i-a]=0
       mask = (b[a:] == 1) & (b[:-a] == 0)
       if no true: return None
       i_abs = a + idx
       q = i_abs; p = i_abs - a
   if l>=0: q=i; p=i+l
   if p==q return None
   # ensure b[p]==0,b[q]==1
   return p,q
```

Pseudo `binary_hill`:

def binary_hill(h_in, n, deadline, seed=42):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    target=n//2
    b=np.zeros(n,float)
    # threshold keep best target
    idx=np.argsort(h_in)[::-1]
    b[idx[:target]]=1.0
    omh=1.0-b
    C=np.correlate(b, 1.0-b, mode='full')
    maxc=float(C.max())
    best_b=b.copy(); bestC=C.copy(); bestmax=maxc
    it=0
    no_imp=0
    # temperature schedule
    T = 0.0
    phase_strict = 20000 or time? We can use no_imp
    while time.time() < deadline - 2:
       # choose p,q
       if rng.random() < 0.7:
           # targeted max lag(s)
           p,q = targeted(b,C,n,rng)
       else:
           # random
           p = rng.randint(n)
           while b[p] > 0.5: p=rng.randint(n)
           q = rng.randint(n)
           while b[q] < 0.5: q=rng.randint(n)
       if p is None: random...
       if p==q: continue
       tri = trial...
       newmax = float(tri.max())
       if newmax < maxc - 1e-12:
          accept; no_imp=0; if newmax<bestmax: update best
       elif newmax <= bestmax + 1e-12 and rng.random()<0.1? # maybe accept equal? no
       else:
          no_imp +=1
       if no_imp > 5000:
          # if T==0, allow T=1 for 2000 iterations, else extra random restart?
          if T ==0.0:
             T=1.0; no_imp=0
             # maybe randomize some swaps for 500 iters? handled by T
          else:
             # restart from best with small perturbation
             b=best_b.copy(); omh=1-b; C=best_C.copy(); maxc=bestmax
             T=0.0
             no_imp=0
       # if T>0: acceptance with prob for worse
       # adjust: if T>0 and newmax <= maxc + T: accept if rand<exp(-(newmax-maxc)/T)
```

`targeted_pair` implementation in top-level:
```
def targeted_swap_pair(b, C, n, rng):
    maxc = C.max()
    # pick 3 candidate lags near max
    for _ in range(5):
        # use cand where C >= maxc - 2
        cand = np.flatnonzero(C >= maxc - 2.0)
        if cand.size == 0:
            cand = np.array([int(np.argmax(C))])
        m = int(cand[rng.randint(cand.size)])
        l = n - 1 - m
        if l == 0:
            continue
        if l > 0:
            seg = n - l
            if seg <=0: continue
            diff = b[:seg] - b[l:n]
            pos = np.flatnonzero(diff > 0.5)
            if pos.size == 0:
                continue
            i = int(pos[rng.randint(pos.size)])
            return (i + l, i)  # p zero, q one
        else:
            a = -l
            seg = n - a
            if seg <=0: continue
            diff = b[a:] - b[:-a]
            pos = np.flatnonzero(diff > 0.5)
            if pos.size ==0: continue
            i = int(a + pos[rng.randint(pos.size)])
            return (i - a, i)
    return (None, None)
```
Random pair:
```
def random_swap_pair(b,n,rng):
    while True:
       p=rng.randint(n); q=rng.randint(n)
       if b[p]<0.5 and b[q]>0.5 and p!=q: return p,q
```
Could infinite if n/2? no.

`trial_swap(C,h,omh,p,q,n)`: If use h binary float, h= b. omh=1-b.
```
def trial_swap(C, h, omh, p, q, n):
    L=2*n-1
    rev=omh[::-1]
    t=C.copy()
    t[p:n+p] += rev
    t[n-1-p:2*n-1-p] -= h
    t[q:n+q] -= rev
    t[n-1-q:2*n-1-q] += h
    t[n-1] -= 2.0
    d = q - p
    m1 = n - 1 - d
    if 0 <= m1 < L: t[m1] += 1.0
    m2 = n - 1 + d
    if 0 <= m2 < L: t[m2] += 1.0
    return t
```
Check for p/q where slices maybe overlapping; in-place order could matter if slices overlap? They modify same array, but addition operations commute if no aliasing? Slices may overlap, and we add/subtract arrays; if overlap and operations sequential, final sum is sum of contributions, independent. No issue.
But `rev=omh[::-1]` is same for p and q. For p addition, as derived: first segment m=p..n+p-1 gets omh reversed. second m=n-1-p..2n-p-1 subtract h forward. For q subtract grad: first segment q..n+q-1 subtract omh_rev; second add h. Good.

After accept, update h and omh:
```
h[p] = 1.0; h[q] = 0.0; omh[p] = 0.0; omh[q] = 1.0
C = t
maxc = float(t.max())
```

Implement in `binary_hill`:
```
def binary_hill(h_in, n, deadline, seed=0, limit_count=10**9):
   ... local_best_count = maxC
   threshold = min(int(limit_count), int(local_best_count)-1)
   if threshold < 0: threshold = 0
   curcnt = count_gt(C, threshold)
   cur_e = curcnt*100.0 + maxc
   ... on local_best update:
       local_best_count = maxc
       threshold = min(int(limit_count), int(local_best_count)-1)
       if threshold <0: threshold=0
       curcnt = count_gt(C, threshold)
       cur_e = curcnt*100.0 + maxc
```

import numpy as np
import time

def c5_value(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full'))) * (2.0/n)

def count_gt(C, threshold): return int(np.count_nonzero(C > threshold + 0.5))

Implement `targeted_pair` carefully to avoid allocating boolean full if threshold high? It's fine.
```
def targeted_pair(b, C, n, threshold, rng):
    L=2*n-1
    high = np.flatnonzero(C > threshold + 0.5)
    if high.size == 0:
        high = np.flatnonzero(C >= C.max() - 1.0)
    if high.size == 0:
        high = np.array([int(np.argmax(C))])
    for _ in range(high.size):
        m = int(high[rng.randint(high.size)])
        l = n - 1 - m
        if l == 0: continue
        if l > 0:
            seg = n - l
            if seg <= 0: continue
            diff = b[:seg] - b[l:n]
            pos = np.flatnonzero(diff > 0.5)
            if pos.size == 0: continue
            i = int(pos[rng.randint(pos.size)])
            return (i + l, i)
        else:
            a = -l
            seg = n - a
            if seg <= 0: continue
            diff = b[a:] - b[:-a]
            pos = np.flatnonzero(diff > 0.5)
            if pos.size == 0: continue
            i = int(a + pos[rng.randint(pos.size)])
            return (i - a, i)
    return (None, None)
```
If high contains many, each iteration scans; caller may call once per proposal. If returns None, caller random. Good.

```

Binary_hill details:
```
def binary_hill(h_in, n, deadline, seed=0, limit_count=1000000, use_as_is=False):
    if n < 4 or n % 2 !=0: return h_in, c5_value...?
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    target = n//2
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if use_as_is:
       b = h_in.astype(float).copy()
       b[b < 0.5]=0.0; b[b >=0.5]=1.0
       if b.sum() != n/2: # adjust
           idx=np.argsort(h_in)[::-1]; b=...
    else:
       idx=np.argsort(h_in)[::-1]; b[idx[:target]]=1.0
    if b.sum() != target: # fix by sorting
       idx=np.where(b==1)[0]; ...
    omh=1.0-b
    C=np.correlate(b, 1.0-b, mode='full')
    maxc=float(C.max())
    best_b=b.copy(); bestC=C.copy(); best_count=maxc
    limit_i=int(limit_count) if limit_count is not None else 10**9
    threshold = min(limit_i, int(best_count)-1)
    if threshold <0: threshold=0
    curcnt = count_gt(C, threshold)
    curE = curcnt*100.0 + maxc
    T=0.0
    no_imp=0
    t_count=0
    restart_count=0
    # local variables for speed? Use while.
    while time.time() < deadline:
        # choose pair 70 targeted
        p=q=None
        if C.size and rng.random() < 0.75:
            p,q = targeted_pair(b,C,n,threshold,rng)
        if p is None:
            p,q = random_pair(b,n,rng)
        if p is None:
            continue
        # Ensure p zero, q one; if not swap assignments? For random, ensured. For targeted, ensured but due float? Check.
        if b[p] > 0.5:
            p,q = q,p
        if b[q] < 0.5 or p==q: continue
        t = trial_swap(C,b,omh,p,q,n)
        newmax=float(t.max())
        newcnt=count_gt(t,threshold)
        newE = newcnt*100.0 + newmax
        accept=False
        if newE < curE - 1e-9:
            accept=True
        elif T > 0.0:
            delta = newE - curE
            if delta <= 0.0:
                accept=True
            else:
                # only allow modest worsen energy, cap to avoid exp underflow
                if delta < 500.0:
                    if rng.random() < np.exp(-delta / T):
                        accept=True
        if accept:
            # apply
            b[p]=1.0; b[q]=0.0
            omh[p]=0.0; omh[q]=1.0
            C=t
            maxc=newmax
            curcnt=newcnt
            curE=newE
            if T > 0.0:
                t_count += 1
                if t_count > 4000:
                    T=0.0; t_count=0
            no_imp=0
            if maxc < best_count - 1e-9:
                best_count=maxc
                best_b=b.copy(); best_C=C.copy()
                # update threshold limit: try lower
                threshold = min(limit_i, int(best_count)-1)
                if threshold < 0: threshold=0
                curcnt = count_gt(C, threshold)
                curE = curcnt*100.0 + maxc
            elif curE < curE... no
        else:
            no_imp += 1
        if no_imp > 10000 and T == 0.0:
            T = 80.0
            t_count=0
            no_imp=0
        if no_imp > 30000:
            # reset to best and perturb
            b = best_b.copy()
            omh = 1.0 - b
            C = best_C.copy()
            maxc = best_count
            curcnt = count_gt(C, threshold)
            curE = curcnt*100.0 + maxc
            no_imp=0
            # perturb if threshold? do deterministic random swaps? Could accept unconditionally but maybe not
            for _ in range(20):
                rp,rq=random_pair(b,n,rng)
                if rp is None: break
                if b[rp] >0.5: rp,rq=rq,rp
                if b[rq] <0.5 or rp==rq: continue
                Pt = trial_swap(C,b,omh,rp,rq,n)
                b[rp]=1; b[rq]=0; omh...; C=Pt; maxc=float(C.max())
            curcnt=count_gt(C,threshold); curE=curcnt*100.0 + maxc
            # If after perturbation worse, keep best tracked; best arrays unchanged
            restart_count +=1
            if restart_count > 20:
               # maybe stop using temperature? continue but avoid infinite? no break maybe
               pass
    if best_count * 2.0/n < c5_value(b,n)? Actually best_b.
    return best_b, float(best_count * 2.0 / n)
```

`random_pair`: Need choose p zero q one. Implement:
```
def random_pair(b,n,rng):
   ones_count = int(b.sum())
   if ones_count ==0 or ones_count==n: return None,None
   for _ in range(100):
      p=int(rng.randint(n)); q=int(rng.randint(n))
      if b[p] <0.5 and b[q] >0.5 and p!=q: return p,q
   # fallback: find arrays once
   ps = np.flatnonzero(b <0.5); qs=np.flatnonzero(b >0.5)
   if ps.size==0 or qs.size==0: return None,None
   return int(ps[rng.randint(ps.size)]), int(qs[rng.randint(qs.size)])
```
Good.

Now, continuous projection helper for initial:
```
def project_feasible(v,n):
    target=n/2.0
    if target==0: return np.zeros(n)
    lo=float(v.min())-1.0; hi=float(v.max())+1.0
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       s=float(np.clip(v-mid,0.0,1.0).sum())
       if s > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    lam=(lo+hi)*0.5
    x=np.clip(v-lam,0.0,1.0)
    # small fix
    for _ in range(50):
       diff=target-x.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-x; s=room.sum()
          if s<=0: break
          x += diff*room/s
       else:
          s=x.sum()
          if s<=0: break
          x += diff*x/s
    return np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)
```
But if v from interpolate, target maybe. Good.

`resample_array`:
```
def resample_array(h_src,n_src,n_out):
    if n_out == n_src: return h_src.astype(float).copy()
    if n_src > n_out and n_src % n_out == 0:
       return h_src.reshape(n_out, n_src//n_out).mean(axis=1)
    if n_src < n_out and n_out % n_src ==0:
       rep = np.repeat(h_src, n_out//n_src); return np.interp? # repeat step? For upsample, linear interp better.
    x_src=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_src); x_out=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n_out)
    return np.interp(x_out, x_src, h_src)
```
For n_src=400,n_out=300, linear interp.
For downsample 400->300 not divide, use interp. It may not preserve sum, but binary conversion count fixes.

def perturb_binary(b, n, rng, swaps=20):
    b=b.astype(float).copy()
    if b.sum()!=n//2: threshold
    h=b; omh=1-b; C=np.correlate(...)
    for _ in range(swaps):
       p,q=random_pair(b,n,rng)
       if p is None: break
       if b[p]>0.5: p,q=q,p
       if b[q]<0.5 or p==q: continue
       C=trial_swap(C,b,omh,p,q,n)
       b[p]=1; b[q]=0; omh[p]=0; omh[q]=1
    return b
```

Top-level `sweep_lag(b, C, n, l, threshold, rng=None)`:
```
def sweep_lag(b, C, n, l, threshold):
    omh=1-b
    best_e = count_gt(C,threshold)*100 + C.max()
    best_pq=None; bestC=None
    if l>0:
       seg=n-l
       pos=np.flatnonzero(b[:seg] - b[l:n] >0.5)
       for i in pos:
          p=i+l; q=i
          t=trial_swap(C,b,omh,p,q,n)
          e=count_gt(t,threshold)*100 + t.max()
          if e < best_e:
             best_e=e; bestC=t; best_pq=(p,q)
    else:
       a=-l; pos=np.flatnonzero(b[a:]-b[:-a]>0.5)
       for i in pos:
          i_abs=a+i; p=i_abs-a; q=i_abs
          ...
    if best_pq is not None: apply and return b,omh,C,best_e
    return None
```

Let's draft code in head:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def int_count(x):
    return int(np.floor(float(x) + 0.5))

def project_feasible(v,n):
    target = n/2.0
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    lo = float(v.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(v.max()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
       mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
       s = float(np.clip(v-mid,0.0,1.0).sum())
       if s > target: lo = mid
       else: hi = mid
    x = np.clip(v - 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(30):
       diff = target - float(x.sum())
       if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
       if diff > 0:
          room = 1.0 - x
          s = float(room.sum())
          if s <= 1e-14: break
          x += frac? x = x + diff * room / s
       else:
          s = float(x.sum())
          if s <= 1e-14: break
          x += frac? x = x + diff * x / s
    return np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)
```
In update, `x += diff * room / s`. This distributes proportionally; may push some above1? Clip after. Might still not exact if all room on saturated? Iterate. Good.

`c5_value`:
```
def c5_value(h,n):
   if len(h)!=n: n=len(h)
   c = np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')
   return float(np.max(c)) * (2.0/float(n))
```

def count_gt(C, threshold):

`perturb_binary`:
```
def perturb_binary(b, n, rng, swaps=30):
    c = np.asarray(b, dtype=float).copy()
    target = n//2
    if abs(c.sum()-target) > 1e-9:
       c = (c > 0.5).astype(float)
       if abs(c.sum()-target) > 1e-9:
          idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1]; c=np.zeros(n); c[idx[:target]]=1
    for _ in range(int(swaps)):
       p=q=None
       for _ in range(20):
          p=int(rng.randint(n)); q=int(rng.randint(n))
          if c[p] < 0.5 and c[q] > 0.5 and p != q: break
       if p is None or c[p] >0.5 or c[q] <0.5: continue
       c[p]=1.0; c[q]=0.0
    return c
```
Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time


def int_count(x):
    return int(np.floor(float(x) + 0.5))


def c5_value(h, n):
    if n != len(h):
        n = len(h)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c)) * (2.0 / float(n))


def count_gt(c, threshold):
    return int(np.count_nonzero(c > (threshold + 0.5)))


def is_binary_array(v, n):
    if len(v) != n:
        return False
    return float(np.max(np.abs(v * (1.0 - v)))) < 1e-10


def project_feasible(v, n):
    target = n / 2.0
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float)
    lo = float(v.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(v.max()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(60):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.clip(v - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    x = np.clip(v - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(40):
        diff = target - float(x.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - x
            s = float(room.sum())
            if s <= 1e-14:
                break
            x += diff * room / s
        else:
            s = float(x.sum())
            if s <= 1e-14:
                break
            x += diff * x / s
    return np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)


def trial_swap(c, h, omh, p, q, n):
    p = int(p)
    q = int(q)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    t = c.copy()
    rev = omh[::-1]

    t[p:n + p] += rev
    t[n - 1 - p:2 * n - 1 - p] -= h

    t[q:n + q] -= rev
    t[n - 1 - q:2 * n - 1 - q] += h

    t[n - 1] -= 2.0
    d = q - p
    m1 = n - 1 - d
    m2 = n - 1 + d
    if 0 <= m1 < L:
        t[m1] += 1.0
    if 0 <= m2 < L:
        t[m2] += 1.0
    return t


def random_pair(b, n, rng):
    for _ in range(30):
        p = int(rng.randint(n))
        q = int(rng.randint(n))
        if b[p] < 0.5 and b[q] > 0.5 and p != q:
            return p, q
    ps = np.flatnonzero(b < 0.5)
    qs = np.flatnonzero(b > 0.5)
    if ps.size == 0 or qs.size == 0:
        return None, None
    return int(ps[rng.randint(ps.size)]), int(qs[rng.randint(qs.size)])


def targeted_pair(b, c, n, threshold, rng):
    high = np.flatnonzero(c > threshold + 0.5)
    if high.size == 0:
        hi = float(c.max()) - 1.0
        high = np.flatnonzero(c >= hi)
    if high.size == 0:
        high = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])

    for _ in range(high.size):
        m = int(high[rng.randint(high.size)])
        lag = n - 1 - m
        if lag == 0:
            continue
        if lag > 0:
            seg = n - lag
            if seg <= 0:
                continue
            diff = b[:seg] - b[lag:n]
            pos = np.flatnonzero(diff > 0.5)
            if pos.size == 0:
                continue
            i = int(pos[rng.randint(pos.size)])
            return i + lag, i
        else:
            a = -lag
            seg = n - a
            if seg <= 0:
                continue
            diff = b[a:] - b[:-a]
            pos = np.flatnonzero(diff > 0.5)
            if pos.size == 0:
                continue
            i = int(a + pos[rng.randint(pos.size)])
            return i - a, i
    return None, None


def sweep_candidates(b, c, n, omh, lag, threshold, rng, maxcand=40):
    if lag == 0:
        return None
    if lag > 0:
        seg = n - lag
        if seg <= 0:
            return None
        pos = np.flatnonzero(b[:seg] - b[lag:n] > 0.5)
        if pos.size == 0:
            return None
        if pos.size > maxcand:
            pos = rng.choice(pos, size=maxcand, replace=False)
    else:
        a = -lag
        seg = n - a
        if seg <= 0:
            return None
        pos = np.flatnonzero(b[a:] - b[:-a] > 0.5)
        if pos.size == 0:
            return None
        if pos.size > maxcand:
            pos = rng.choice(pos, size=maxcand, replace=False)

    best_e = None
    best = None
    for r in pos:
        r = int(r)
        if lag > 0:
            p, q = r + lag, r
        else:
            p, q = r, r + a
        t = trial_swap(c, b, omh, p, q, n)
        nm = float(t.max())
        nc = count_gt(t, threshold)
        e = nc * 100.0 + nm
        if best_e is None or e < best_e:
            best_e = e
            best = (t, p, q, nm, nc)
    return best


def binary_hill(h_in, n, deadline, seed=0, limit_count=1000000, use_as_is=False, stop_at_limit=False):
    if n < 4 or n % 2 != 0:
        hv = np.clip(np.asarray(h_in, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
        nv = len(hv)
        return hv, c5_value(hv, nv), 0

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    target = n // 2
    b = np.asarray(h_in, dtype=float).copy()

    if use_as_is:
        b[b < 0.5] = 0.0
        b[b >= 0.5] = 1.0

    if abs(float(b.sum()) - target) > 1e-9:
        idx = np.argsort(h_in)[::-1]
        b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        idx2 = idx[:n] if len(idx) >= n else idx
        b[idx2[:target]] = 1.0

    omh = 1.0 - b
    c = np.correlate(b, 1.0 - b, mode="full").astype(float)
    maxc = float(c.max())
    best_b = b.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_count = int_count(maxc)
    limit_i = int(limit_count)

    if stop_at_limit and maxc <= limit_i + 0.5:
        return best_b, float(maxc * 2.0 / n), best_count

    threshold = min(limit_i, best_count - 1)
    if threshold < 0:
        threshold = 0
    curcnt = count_gt(c, threshold)
    cure = curcnt * 100.0 + maxc

    T = 0.0
    no_imp = 0
    t_count = 0
    accepted = 0
    sweep_cd = 0
    resets = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        if sweep_cd > 0:
            sweep_cd -= 1

        p = q = None
        if rng.random() < 0.78:
            p, q = targeted_pair(b, c, n, threshold, rng)
        if p is None:
            p, q = random_pair(b, n, rng)

        if p is None:
            continue
        if b[p] > 0.5:
            p, q = q, p
        if b[q] < 0.5 or p == q:
            continue

        t = trial_swap(c, b, omh, p, q, n)
        newmax = float(t.max())
        newcnt = count_gt(t, threshold)
        nowe = newcnt * 100.0 + newmax

        do = False
        if nowe < cure - 1e-9:
            do = True
        elif T > 0.0:
            delta = nowe - cure
            if delta <= 0.0:
                do = True
            elif delta < 250.0 and rng.random() < np.exp(-delta / T):
                do = True

        if do:
            b[p] = 1.0
            b[q] = 0.0
            omh[p] = 0.0
            omh[q] = 1.0
            c = t
            maxc = newmax
            curcnt = newcnt
            cure = nowe
            accepted += 1
            if T > 0.0:
                t_count += 1
                if t_count > 4000:
                    T = 0.0
                    t_count = 0
            no_imp = 0

            if accepted % 5000 == 0:
                c = np.correlate(b, 1.0 - b, mode="full").astype(float)
                maxc = float(c.max())
                curcnt = count_gt(c, threshold)
                cure = curcnt * 100.0 + maxc
                accepted = 0

            nb = int_count(maxc)
            if nb < best_count:
                best_count = nb
                best_b = b.copy()
                best_c = c.copy()
                threshold = min(limit_i, best_count - 1)
                if threshold < 0:
                    threshold = 0
                curcnt = count_gt(c, threshold)
                cure = curcnt * 100.0 + maxc
                if stop_at_limit and best_count <= limit_i:
                    break
        else:
            no_imp += 1

        if no_imp > 6000 and sweep_cd == 0:
            hi = np.flatnonzero(c > threshold + 0.5)
            if hi.size:
                m = int(hi[0])
                lag = n - 1 - m
                res = sweep_candidates(b, c, n, omh, lag, threshold, rng, 40)
                if res is not None and (res[3] * 1.0) > 0.0:
                    tt, pp, qq, nm, nc = res
                    ne = nc * 100.0 + nm
                    if ne < cure - 1e-9:
                        b[pp] = 1.0
                        b[qq] = 0.0
                        omh[pp] = 0.0
                        omh[qq] = 1.0
                        c = tt
                        maxc = nm
                        curcnt = nc
                        cure = ne
                        no_imp = 0
                        nb = int_count(maxc)
                        if nb < best_count:
                            best_count = nb
                            best_b = b.copy()
                            best_c = c.copy()
                            threshold = min(limit_i, best_count - 1)
                            if threshold < 0:
                                threshold = 0
                            curcnt = count_gt(c, threshold)
                            cure = curcnt * 100.0 + maxc
                            if stop_at_limit and best_count <= limit_i:
                                break
            sweep_cd = 150

        if no_imp > 10000 and T == 0.0:
            T = 55.0
            t_count = 0
            no_imp = 0

        if no_imp > 40000:
            b = best_b.copy()
            omh = 1.0 - b
            c = best_c.copy()
            maxc = float(best_count)
            curcnt = count_gt(c, threshold)
            cure = curcnt * 100.0 + maxc
            no_imp = 0
            for _ in range(12):
                rp, rq = random_pair(b, n, rng)
                if rp is None:
                    break
                if b[rp] > 0.5:
                    rp, rq = rq, rp
                if b[rq] < 0.5 or rp == rq:
                    continue
                c = trial_swap(c, b, omh, rp, rq, n)
                b[rp] = 1.0
                b[rq] = 0.0
                omh[rp] = 0.0
                omh[rq] = 1.0
            maxc = float(c.max())
            curcnt = count_gt(c, threshold)
            cure = curcnt * 100.0 + maxc
            resets += 1
            if resets > 25:
                T = 0.0

    best_count = int(best_count)
    return best_b, float(best_count * 2.0 / n), best_count


def resample_array(h_src, n_src, n_out):
    if n_src == n_out:
        return np.asarray(h_src, dtype=float).copy()
    if n_src > n_out and n_src % n_out == 0:
        return np.asarray(h_src, dtype=float).reshape(n_out, n_src // n_out).mean(axis=1)
    xs = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_src)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_out)
    return np.interp(xo, xs, np.asarray(h_src, dtype=float))


def make_random_binary(n, seed):
    r = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    b = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    b[r.permutation(n)[:n // 2]] = 1.0
    return b


def perturb_binary(b, n, rng, swaps=25):
    c = np.asarray(b, dtype=float).copy()
    target = n // 2
    if abs(float(c.sum()) - target) > 1e-9:
        idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1]
        c = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        c[idx[:target]] = 1.0
    for _ in range(int(swaps)):
        p = q = None
        for _ in range(20):
            p = int(rng.randint(n))
            q = int(rng.randint(n))
            if c[p] < 0.5 and c[q] > 0.5 and p != q:
                break
        if p is None or c[p] > 0.5 or c[q] < 0.5:
            continue
        c[p] = 1.0
        c[q] = 0.0
    return c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total = max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 15.0)
    end = t0 + total

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n0 = int(len(h0))
    except Exception:
        n0 = 400
        h0 = np.full(n0, 0.5, dtype=float)

    if n0 < 10:
        n0 = 400
        h0 = np.full(n0, 0.5, dtype=float)
    if n0 % 2 != 0:
        n0 -= 1
        h0 = h0[:n0]
    h0 = project_feasible(h0, n0)

    best_h = h0.copy()
    best_c5 = c5_value(best_h, n0)
    best_n = n0

    binary_by_n = {}
    bin_c5 = {}
    bin_count = {}

    ns = []
    for raw in (n0, (3 * n0) // 4, n0 // 2, (5 * n0) // 4, 300, 200, 400):
        nv = int(raw)
        if nv % 2 == 1:
            nv += 1
        if 16 <= nv <= 1000 and nv not in ns:
            ns.append(nv)

    if len(ns) <= 2:
        fracs = [0.55, 0.25, 0.10, 0.05]
    elif len(ns) == 3:
        fracs = [0.45, 0.25, 0.15, 0.10]
    else:
        fracs = [0.40, 0.22, 0.16, 0.12]

    cur_frac = 0.0
    phase = 0
    for nv in ns:
        frac = fracs[phase] if phase < len(fracs) else 0.05
        phase += 1
        cur_frac += frac
        if cur_frac > 0.88 and phase > 1:
            break
        dl = min(t0 + cur_frac * total, end - 5.0)
        if dl <= time.time() + 5.0:
            continue

        if nv in binary_by_n and bin_c5.get(nv, best_c5) <= best_c5 + 1e-12:
            src = binary_by_n[nv]
            use = True
        else:
            src = best_h
            use = is_binary_array(src, nv)

        h_work = resample_array(src, len(src), nv)
        limit = int(np.floor(best_c5 * nv / 2.0 + 1e-9))
        if limit < 0:
            limit = 0
        if limit > nv:
            limit = nv

        bh, bc, cnt = binary_hill(
            h_work, nv, dl,
            seed=seed + 17 + phase,
            limit_count=limit,
            use_as_is=use,
            stop_at_limit=True
        )

        if nv not in bin_c5 or bc < bin_c5[nv] - 1e-12:
            binary_by_n[nv] = bh
            bin_c5[nv] = bc
            bin_count[nv] = int(cnt)

        if bc < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_h = bh.copy()
            best_n = nv
            best_c5 = float(bc)

    if best_n in bin_count:
        curcnt = int(bin_count[best_n])
    else:
        curcnt = int(np.floor(best_c5 * best_n / 2.0 + 0.5))

    if best_n in binary_by_n:
        src = binary_by_n[best_n]
        use = True
    else:
        src = best_h
        use = is_binary_array(src, best_n)

    limit_lower = max(0, curcnt - 1)
    if limit_lower >= 0 and time.time() < end - 25:
        h_work = resample_array(src, len(src), best_n)
        dl = min(end - 20.0, time.time() + 45.0)
        bh, bc, cnt = binary_hill(
            h_work, best_n, dl,
            seed=seed + 91,
            limit_count=limit_lower,
            use_as_is=use,
            stop_at_limit=True
        )
        if bc < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_h = bh.copy()
            best_n = best_n
            best_c5 = float(bc)
            bin_c5[best_n] = bc
            bin_count[best_n] = int(cnt)
            binary_by_n[best_n] = bh

    rnd = np.random.RandomState(seed + 555)
    restart = 0
    cand = [best_n]
    for nv in ns:
        if nv != best_n:
            cand.append(nv)
    if not cand:
        cand = [best_n]

    while time.time() < end - 3.0:
        nv = cand[restart % len(cand)]
        restart += 1

        if nv in binary_by_n and bin_c5.get(nv, best_c5) <= best_c5 + 1e-12:
            use = True
            h_start = perturb_binary(binary_by_n[nv], nv, rnd, 12 + (restart % 36))
        else:
            use = False
            h_work = resample_array(best_h, len(best_h), nv)
            if rnd.random() < 0.30:
                h_start = make_random_binary(nv, seed + restart + 1234)
            else:
                h_start = h_work

        limit_g = int(np.floor(best_c5 * nv / 2.0 + 1e-9))
        if limit_g < 0:
            limit_g = 0
        if limit_g > nv:
            limit_g = nv
        limit = limit_g
        if nv in bin_count:
            lc = int(bin_count[nv])
            if lc * 2.0 / nv <= best_c5 + 1e-9 and limit_g > 0:
                limit = max(0, lc - 1)

        dl = min(end - 2.0, time.time() + 18.0 + float((restart % 4)) * 7.0)
        bh, bc, cnt = binary_hill(
            h_start, nv, dl,
            seed=seed + restart + 5000,
            limit_count=limit,
            use_as_is=use,
            stop_at_limit=True
        )

        if nv not in bin_c5 or bc < bin_c5[nv] - 1e-12:
            binary_by_n[nv] = bh
            bin_c5[nv] = bc
            bin_count[nv] = int(cnt)

        if bc < best_c5 - 1e-12:
            best_h = bh.copy()
            best_n = nv
            best_c5 = float(bc)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    if np.all((best_h < 0.02) | (best_h > 0.98)):
        idx = np.argsort(best_h)[::-1]
        b = np.zeros(best_n, dtype=float)
        b[idx[:best_n // 2]] = 1.0
        best_h = b
    best_c5 = c5_value(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, float(best_c5), int(best_n)
```
